Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AT2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : 01
Hangi Tablo için : MAKR7S
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Sevk Ýrsaliyelerininde dos/dinamo beraber kullanýmýndan kaynaklanan problem Düzeltmeye çalýþan makro
Kod : 
Dim Kaydedilsinmi
Dim TablNo
Sub Do2(Doc1,Tbl1,SatirNo)

	If TablNo=32 Then
		If	Doc1.IsBlank(Tbl1.REAL_SIPNO(SatirNo)) Or Not Doc1.IsBlank(Tbl1.REAL_SIPARTNO(SatirNo)) Then Exit Sub
	End If

	Dim Tbl01
	Dim Kriter

	Kriter="KOD='"
	Kriter=Kriter&Tbl1.KOD(SatirNo)
	Kriter=Kriter&"'"

	Kriter=Kriter&"&SERINO='"
	Kriter=Kriter&Tbl1.SERINO(SatirNo)
	Kriter=Kriter&"'"

	Kriter=Kriter&"&LOTNUMBER='"
	Kriter=Kriter&Tbl1.LOTNUMBER(SatirNo)
	Kriter=Kriter&"'"

	Kriter=Kriter&"&AMBCODE='"
	Kriter=Kriter&Tbl1.AMBCODE(SatirNo)
	Kriter=Kriter&"'"

	Set Tbl01=Doc1.Select1("_STOK01_345345","STOK01",Kriter,"",-1)
	Tbl01.GroupBy("ALLOC_SYSSTATUS+ALLOC_NONMOVABL+ALLOC_SIPTYPE+ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE+ALLOC_EVRAKNO+ALLOC_SIPNO+ALLOC_SIPARTNO+ALLOC_MPSEVNO+ALLOC_MPSARTNO")

	Dim ALLOC_SIPARTNO
	
	Dim I
	Set Tbl01=Doc1.Select2("_STOK01_345345_2","_STOK01_345345","SF_MIKTAR<>0","",-1)
	If TablNo=32 Then
		Tbl01.GroupBy("ALLOC_SIPNO+ALLOC_SIPARTNO")
		If Tbl01.GetRowCount()=2 Then
				ALLOC_SIPARTNO=Tbl01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(1)
				If Doc1.IsBlank(ALLOC_SIPARTNO) Then ALLOC_SIPARTNO=Tbl01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(2)
				If Tbl1.REAL_SIPNO(SatirNo)=Tbl01.ALLOC_SIPNO(1) And Tbl1.REAL_SIPARTNO(SatirNo)<>ALLOC_SIPARTNO Then
					Tbl1.REAL_SIPARTNO(SatirNo)=ALLOC_SIPARTNO
					If Tbl1.REAL_SIPARTNO(SatirNo)<>Tbl1.SIPARTNO(SatirNo) Then	
						Tbl1.FARKLIDAGITIM(SatirNo)="*"
					End If
					Kaydedilsinmi=1
				End If
		End If
		Exit Sub
	End If


	If TablNo=30001 Then
		If Tbl01.GetRowCount()=4 Then
				Dim MDCount
				Dim SVCount
				MDCount=0
				SVCount=0
				If Tbl01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(1)="MD" Then MDCount=MDCount+1
				If Tbl01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(2)="MD" Then MDCount=MDCount+1
				If Tbl01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(3)="MD" Then MDCount=MDCount+1
				If Tbl01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(4)="MD" Then MDCount=MDCount+1
				If Tbl01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(1)="SV" Then SVCount=SVCount+1
				If Tbl01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(2)="SV" Then SVCount=SVCount+1
				If Tbl01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(3)="SV" Then SVCount=SVCount+1
				If Tbl01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(4)="SV" Then SVCount=SVCount+1
				If SVCount<>2 Or MDCount<>2 Then Exit Sub
				Tbl01.GroupBy("ALLOC_SIPNO+ALLOC_SIPARTNO")
				If Tbl01.GetRowCount()<>2 Then Exit Sub
				ALLOC_SIPARTNO=Tbl01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(1)
				If Doc1.IsBlank(ALLOC_SIPARTNO) Then ALLOC_SIPARTNO=Tbl01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(2)

				If Tbl1.OALLOC_SIPNO(SatirNo)=Tbl01.ALLOC_SIPNO(1) And Tbl1.OALLOC_SIPARTNO(SatirNo)<>ALLOC_SIPARTNO Then
					Tbl1.OALLOC_SIPARTNO(SatirNo)=ALLOC_SIPARTNO
					Kaydedilsinmi=1
				End If
		End If
		Exit Sub
	End If



	If Tbl01.GetRowCount()=2 Then
		If Doc1.IsAlmostZero(Tbl01.SF_MIKTAR(1)+Tbl01.SF_MIKTAR(2)) Then
			Dim EI
			Dim PI
			EI=0
			PI=0
			If Tbl01.SF_MIKTAR(1)<0 Then EI=1
			If Tbl01.SF_MIKTAR(2)<0 Then EI=2
			If Tbl01.SF_MIKTAR(1)>0 Then PI=1
			If Tbl01.SF_MIKTAR(2)>0 Then PI=2
			If EI>0 And PI>0 Then
				If Doc1.IsAlmostEqual(Tbl01.SF_MIKTAR(PI),Tbl1.SF_MIKTAR(SatirNo)) Then
					Tbl1.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(SatirNo)=Tbl01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(PI)
					Tbl1.OALLOC_EVRAKNO(SatirNo)=Tbl01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(PI)
					Tbl1.OALLOC_SIPARTNO(SatirNo)=Tbl01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(PI)
					If TablNo=28 Then
						Tbl1.OALLOC_SIPNO(SatirNo)=Tbl01.ALLOC_SIPNO(PI)
						Tbl1.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS(SatirNo)=Tbl01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(PI)
						Tbl1.OALLOC_NONMOVABL(SatirNo)=Tbl01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(PI)
						Tbl1.OALLOC_SIPTYPE	(SatirNo)=Tbl01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(PI)

						Tbl1.OALLOC_MPSEVNO	(SatirNo)=Tbl01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(PI)
						Tbl1.OALLOC_MPSARTNO(SatirNo)=Tbl01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(PI)

					End If
					Kaydedilsinmi=1
				End If
			End If
		End If
	End If
End Sub

Sub Do1(Doc1,Tbl1)
	Dim I
	Dim nret
	nret=Doc1.LoadFirst_Voucher()
	While nret=0		
		Kaydedilsinmi=0
		For I=1 To Tbl1.GetRowCount
			Call Do2(Doc1,Tbl1,I)
		Next
		If Kaydedilsinmi>0 Then
			If Doc1.Save_Voucher()<>0 Then 
				Doc.d7MsgBox Doc1.GetLoadedVoucher&" Kodlu Kart/Evrak Kaydedilirken Hata","",-1
			End If
		End If
		nret=Doc1.LoadNext_Voucher()
	Wend
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Sub Makro1_Hepsi()
	Dim be
	Set be=Doc.CreateSobject("BatchErrorMode")
	Dim Doc1
	Dim Tbl1

	TablNo=32
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK32","")
	Set Tbl1=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK32T")
	Call Do1(Doc1,Tbl1)
	Set Doc1=Nothing
	Doc.DeleteDocumentNoUI "STOK32",""

	TablNo=30001
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK30","")
	Set Tbl1=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK30T")
	Call Do1(Doc1,Tbl1)
	Set Doc1=Nothing
	Doc.DeleteDocumentNoUI "STOK30",""

	TablNo=30
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK30","")
	Set Tbl1=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK30T")
	Call Do1(Doc1,Tbl1)
	Set Doc1=Nothing
	Doc.DeleteDocumentNoUI "STOK30",""

	TablNo=28
	Set Doc1=Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK28","")
	Set Tbl1=Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK28T")
	Call Do1(Doc1,Tbl1)
	Set Doc1=Nothing
	Doc.DeleteDocumentNoUI "STOK28",""

End sub

Sub Makro1()
	Call Makro1_Hepsi	
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Tamam","",-1
End sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AT2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : AGIRLIK
Hangi Tablo için : STOK66
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Çeki Aðýrlýklarý Hesapla
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to STOK66T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBArMessage "Aðýrlýklar Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & STOK66T.GetRowCount()
		STOK66T.NETAGIRLIK(i) = Doc.Round(Agirlik_Calc(STOK66T.KOD(i), STOK66T.SF_MIKTAR(i)), 6)
		MakaraAgirlik = GetMakaraAgirligi(i)
		STOK66T.BRUTAGIRLIK(i) = STOK66T.NETAGIRLIK(i) + MakaraAgirlik
		ToplamBrut = ToplamBrut + STOK66T.BRUTAGIRLIK(i)
		ToplamNet = ToplamNet + STOK66T.NETAGIRLIK(i)
	Next
	Set S66T = Doc.Select2("sdfsd", "STOK66T", "PACKNO_1>'.'", "", -1)
	S66T.GroupBy("PACKNO_1")
	ToplamBrut = ToplamBrut + S66T.GetRowCount() * 25

	STOK66E.NOTES_1 = "Toplam Brut Aðýrlýk = " & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamBrut,12,0) & " kg"
	STOK66E.NOTES_2 = "Toplam Net Aðýrlýk = " & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamNet,12,0) & " kg"
	k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Toplam Brut Aðýrlýk = " & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamBrut,12,0) & " kg", "", MB_OK+MB_ICONINFORMATION)
End Sub
Function Agirlik_Calc(StokKodu, Miktar)
	Agirlik = 0
	ret=Doc.Calc_Bom("O","M",StokKodu,1,Miktar,"","","","","","","","")
	Set Tbmrsys =Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT")
	for j = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		Birim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j), "")
		if uCase(trim(Birim)) = "KG" Then
			Agirlik = Agirlik + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(j)
		End if
	Next
	Agirlik_Calc = Agirlik
End Function
Function GetMakaraAgirligi(satirNo)
	MakaraAgirlik = 0
	sls = InStr(1, STOK66T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo), "/")
	if sls = 0 Then Exit Function
	Makara = Left(STOK66T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo), sls-1)
	GetMakaraAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("AMBLJ00", "AGIRLIK", Doc.LeftJustify(Makara, 24), "")
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Set S = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	For i = 1 to STOK66T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBArMessage "Aðýrlýklar Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & STOK66T.GetRowCount()
		STOK66T.NETAGIRLIK(i) = Doc.Round(Agirlik_Calc(STOK66T.KOD(i), STOK66T.SF_MIKTAR(i)), 6)
		MakaraAgirlik = GetMakaraAgirligi(i)
		STOK66T.BRUTAGIRLIK(i) = STOK66T.NETAGIRLIK(i) + MakaraAgirlik
		ToplamBrut = ToplamBrut + STOK66T.BRUTAGIRLIK(i)
		ToplamNet = ToplamNet + STOK66T.NETAGIRLIK(i)
	Next
	Set S66T = Doc.Select2("sdfsd", "STOK66T", "PACKNO_1>'.'", "", -1)
	S66T.GroupBy("PACKNO_1")
if trim(STOK66E.MUSTERIKODU) = "000004" Then	
For i = 1 to S66T.GetRowCount()
		if S66T.NETAGIRLIK(i) > 975 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox S66T.PACKNO_1(i) & " no.lu paletin aðrlýðý 1000 kg 'ýn üzerinde", "", MB_OK
		end if
	Next
end if
	ToplamBrut = ToplamBrut + S66T.GetRowCount() * 25

	STOK66E.NOTES_1 = "Toplam Brut Aðýrlýk = " & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamBrut,12,0) & " kg"
	STOK66E.NOTES_2 = "Toplam Net Aðýrlýk = " & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamNet,12,0) & " kg"
	k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Toplam Brut Aðýrlýk = " & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamBrut,12,0) & " kg", "", MB_OK+MB_ICONINFORMATION)
End Sub
Function Agirlik_Calc(StokKodu, Miktar)
	Agirlik = 0
	ret=Doc.Calc_Bom("O","M",StokKodu,1,Miktar,"","","","","","","","")
	Set Tbmrsys =Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT")
	for j = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		Birim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j), "")
		if uCase(trim(Birim)) = "KG" Then
			Agirlik = Agirlik + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(j)
		End if
	Next
	Agirlik_Calc = Agirlik
End Function

	Makara = Left(STOK66T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo), sls-1)
	GetMakaraAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("AMBLJ00", "AGIRLIK", Doc.LeftJustify(Makara, 24), "")
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AT2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : BANKA
Hangi Tablo için : BORDRO1
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : BANKA ÖDEME DOSYASI
Kod : 
Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, satirAra, SatirBitis
Public ToplamEURTutar, ToplamEURIskonto, ToplamEURIskontoluTutar
Public Grid, xCizgi
Public f
Sub Makro1()

	Tarih = doc.Today()
	Gun = Right(Tarih,2)
	Ay = Mid(Tarih,6,2)
	Yil = Left(Tarih,4) 
	dim fso
	set fso = CreateObject("SCRIPTING.FILESYSTEMOBJECT")
	Set f = fso.opentextfile("D:\ÝÞ BANKASI\"&Gun&"."&Ay&"."&yil&"ATOMKAB.txt",2,true,-2)

	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	satirSayisi = BRDR1TP.GetRecCount()

	Tarih = doc.Today()
	Gun = Right(Tarih,2)
	Ay = Mid(Tarih,6,2)
	Yil = Left(Tarih,4) 	
		
	f.writeline("1063200000005536560ATOM KABLO A.Þ.          TRY")
	
	Toplam = 0

	
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Kod = BRDR1TP.KOD(i)
		Sube = doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM0","SUBE_KODU",Kod,"")
		Banka = doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM0","HESAPNO",Kod,"")
		SubeKodu = Left(Sube,4) 
		HesapNo = Left(Banka,7)
		AdSoyad = doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM0","AD_SOYAD",Kod,"")
		Ad = doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM0","AD",Kod,"")
 		Ad = Left(Ad,12)
		SoyAd = doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM0","SOYAD",Kod,"")
 		SoyAd = Left(SoyAd,22)
		Odenecek = BRDR1TP.NETODENECEK(i)
		Odenecek = formatnumber(Odenecek,2,-1,0,0)
		dim strOdenecek
		strOdenecek = cstr(Odenecek)
		strOdenecek = space(15-len(strOdenecek))&strOdenecek
		strOdenecek = replace(strOdenecek," ","0")
		Toplam = Toplam + BRDR1TP.NETODENECEK(i)

		'f.writeline("2H"&SubeKodu&" "&HesapNo&strOdenecek&AdSoyad)
		f.writeline("2H"&SubeKodu & HesapNo & " " & strOdenecek & AdSoyad )

	Next
		Toplam = formatnumber(Toplam,2,-1,0,0)
		dim strToplam
		strToplam = cstr(Toplam)
		strToplam = space(15-len(strToplam))&strToplam
		strToplam = replace(strToplam," ","0")
	f.writeline("3000000000000000"&SatirSayisi&strToplam)

	f.close()


Set fso = nothing
set f = nothing

End Sub

Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, satirAra, SatirBitis
Public ToplamEURTutar, ToplamEURIskonto, ToplamEURIskontoluTutar
Public Grid, xCizgi
Public f
Sub Makro1()

	Tarih = doc.Today()
	Gun = Right(Tarih,2)
	Ay = Mid(Tarih,6,2)
	Yil = Left(Tarih,4) 
	dim fso
	set fso = CreateObject("SCRIPTING.FILESYSTEMOBJECT")
	Set f = fso.opentextfile("D:\ÝÞ BANKASI\"&Gun&"."&Ay&"."&yil&"ATOMKAB.txt",2,true,-2)

	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	satirSayisi = BORDROAT.GetRecCount()

	Tarih = doc.Today()
	Gun = Right(Tarih,2)
	Ay = Mid(Tarih,6,2)
	Yil = Left(Tarih,4) 	
		
	f.writeline("1063200000005536560ATOM KABLO A.Þ.          TRY")
	
	Toplam = 0

	
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Kod = BORDROAT.KOD(i)
		Sube = doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM0","SUBE_KODU",Kod,"")
		Banka = doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM0","HESAPNO",Kod,"")
		SubeKodu = Left(Sube,4) 
		HesapNo = Left(Banka,7)
		AdSoyad = doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM0","AD_SOYAD",Kod,"")
		Ad = doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM0","AD",Kod,"")
 		Ad = Left(Ad,12)
		SoyAd = doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM0","SOYAD",Kod,"")
 		SoyAd = Left(SoyAd,22)
		Odenecek = BORDROAT.OR_BORCFIYAT(i)
		Odenecek = formatnumber(Odenecek,2,-1,0,0)
		dim strOdenecek
		strOdenecek = cstr(Odenecek)
		strOdenecek = space(15-len(strOdenecek))&strOdenecek
		strOdenecek = replace(strOdenecek," ","0")
	
		Toplam = Toplam + BORDROAT.OR_BORCFIYAT(i)

		f.writeline("2H"&SubeKodu & HesapNo & " " & strOdenecek & AdSoyad )

	Next
		Toplam = formatnumber(Toplam,2,-1,0,0)
		dim strToplam
		strToplam = cstr(Toplam)
		strToplam = space(15-len(strToplam))&strToplam
		strToplam = replace(strToplam," ","0")
	f.writeline("3000000000000000"&SatirSayisi&strToplam)

	f.close()


Set fso = nothing
set f = nothing

End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AT2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : BOMAKT
Hangi Tablo için : BOMU01
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Reçete Güncelleme (XLS)
Kod : 
Option Explicit
Sub FromExcel(filename)
	Dim i
	Dim j
	Dim Sh
	Dim Wb
	Dim Excel

	Dim BOMU01T_TEXTDATA
	Set BOMU01T_TEXTDATA=Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01T_TEXTDATA")
	BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.Empty()

	Doc.StatusbarMessage "Excel Açýlýyor"

'	on error resume next

	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Open(filename)
	Set Sh = Wb.Sheets("BOMU01T")

	if Err.Number<>0 then
		Doc.d7MsgBox Err.Number & "->" & Err.Description,"freedom",MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	end if

	Doc.StatusbarMessage "Okunuyor"

	Dim DataIlkSatir
	Dim DataSonSatir
	Dim DataBitisSatiriReferansKolon

	DataIlkSatir=2
	DataBitisSatiriReferansKolon=13

	Dim vval
	for i=DataIlkSatir to 65000
		vval=Sh.Cells(i,DataBitisSatiriReferansKolon)
		if Trim(vval)="" then
			Exit For
		end if
		DataSonSatir=i
	next

	Dim col_EVRAKNO
	Dim col_AKTIF_PASIF
	Dim col_ACIKLAMA
	Dim col_BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR
	Dim col_BOMREC_CODE
	Dim col_BOMREC_ALTKODU
	Dim col_BOMREC_SIRANO
	Dim col_BOMREC_INPUTTYPE
	Dim col_BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE
	Dim col_BOMREC_OPERASYON
	Dim col_BOMREC_KAYNAK0
	Dim col_BOMREC_YMAMULPS
	Dim col_BOMREC_YMAMULPM

	Dim EVRAKNO
	Dim AKTIF_PASIF
	Dim ACIKLAMA
	Dim BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR
	Dim BOMREC_CODE
	Dim BOMREC_ALTKODU
	Dim BOMREC_SIRANO
	Dim BOMREC_INPUTTYPE
	Dim BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE
	Dim BOMREC_OPERASYON
	Dim BOMREC_KAYNAK0
	Dim BOMREC_YMAMULPS
	Dim BOMREC_YMAMULPM

	Dim HATAVAR
	Dim HATAACIKLAMASI

	col_EVRAKNO=10
	col_AKTIF_PASIF=11
	col_ACIKLAMA=12
	col_BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR=13
	col_BOMREC_CODE=14
	col_BOMREC_ALTKODU=16
	col_BOMREC_SIRANO=17
	col_BOMREC_INPUTTYPE=18
	col_BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE=19
	col_BOMREC_OPERASYON=21
	col_BOMREC_KAYNAK0=23
	col_BOMREC_YMAMULPS=25
	col_BOMREC_YMAMULPM=26

	Dim RecNum
	Dim dummyVal

	for i=DataIlkSatir to DataSonSatir
		HATAVAR="0"
		HATAACIKLAMASI=""

		EVRAKNO					=Sh.Cells(i,col_EVRAKNO) & ""
		ACIKLAMA					=Sh.Cells(i,col_ACIKLAMA) & ""
		AKTIF_PASIF				=Sh.Cells(i,col_AKTIF_PASIF) & ""
		BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR	=Sh.Cells(i,col_BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR)
		BOMREC_CODE				=Sh.Cells(i,col_BOMREC_CODE) & ""
		BOMREC_ALTKODU			=Sh.Cells(i,col_BOMREC_ALTKODU) & ""
		BOMREC_SIRANO			=Sh.Cells(i,col_BOMREC_SIRANO) & ""
		BOMREC_INPUTTYPE		=Sh.Cells(i,col_BOMREC_INPUTTYPE) & ""
		BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE		=Sh.Cells(i,col_BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE) & ""
		BOMREC_OPERASYON		=Sh.Cells(i,col_BOMREC_OPERASYON) & ""
		BOMREC_KAYNAK0			=Sh.Cells(i,col_BOMREC_KAYNAK0)
		BOMREC_YMAMULPS		=Sh.Cells(i,col_BOMREC_YMAMULPS)
		BOMREC_YMAMULPM		=Sh.Cells(i,col_BOMREC_YMAMULPM)

		EVRAKNO=Doc.RightJustify(EVRAKNO,10)
		if Trim(EVRAKNO)="" then
			HATAVAR="1"
			HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"[Föy No Boþ]"
		end if

		if BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR=0 then
			HATAVAR="1"
			HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"[Mamul Miktarý=0]"
		end if

		if Trim(BOMREC_CODE)="" then
			HATAVAR="1"
			HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"[Mamul Kodu Boþ]"
		end if

		if Trim(BOMREC_SIRANO)="" then
			HATAVAR="1"
			HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"[Sýra No Boþ Olamaz]"
		end if

		if Trim(BOMREC_INPUTTYPE)="" then
			HATAVAR="1"
			HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"[Kaynak Tipi Boþ Olamaz]"
		end if


		if Trim(BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE)="" then
			HATAVAR="1"
			HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"[Kaynak Kodu Boþ Olamaz]"
		end if

		if Trim(BOMREC_KAYNAK0)=0 then
			HATAVAR="1"
			HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"[Kaynak Miktarý=0]"
		end if

		dummyVal=Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","KOD",BOMREC_CODE," ")
		if Trim(dummyVal)="" then
			HATAVAR="1"
			HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"["+Trim(BOMREC_CODE)+"] Mamul Kodu Tanýmlý Deðil"
		end if

		dummyVal=BOMREC_INPUTTYPE
		if Trim(dummyVal)<>"H" and Trim(dummyVal)<>"I" and Trim(dummyVal)<>"Y"  then
			HATAVAR="1"
			HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"["+Trim(BOMREC_INPUTTYPE)+"] Kaynak Tipi Hatalý"
		end if

		if Trim(BOMREC_INPUTTYPE)="H" or Trim(BOMREC_INPUTTYPE)="Y" then
			dummyVal=Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","KOD",BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE," ")
			if Trim(dummyVal)="" then
				HATAVAR="1"
				HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"["+Trim(BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE)+"] Kaynak Kodu Tanýmlý Deðil"
			end if
		end if

		if Trim(BOMREC_INPUTTYPE)="I" then
			if Trim(BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE)<>"" then
				dummyVal=Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","KOD",BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE," ")
				if Trim(dummyVal)="" then
					HATAVAR="1"
					HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"["+Trim(BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE)+"] Kaynak Kodu Tanýmlý Deðil"
				end if
			end if
			if Trim(BOMREC_OPERASYON)<>"" then
				dummyVal=Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01","KOD",BOMREC_OPERASYON," ")
				if Trim(dummyVal)="" then
					HATAVAR="1"
					HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"["+Trim(BOMREC_OPERASYON)+"] Operasyon Kodu Tanýmlý Deðil"
				end if
			end if
		end if

		RecNum=BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.AddRow()
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.EVRAKNO(RecNum)=EVRAKNO
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.AKTIF_PASIF(RecNum)=AKTIF_PASIF
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.ACIKLAMA(RecNum)=ACIKLAMA
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR(RecNum)=BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.BOMREC_CODE(RecNum)=BOMREC_CODE
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.BOMREC_ALTKODU(RecNum)=BOMREC_ALTKODU
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.BOMREC_SIRANO(RecNum)=BOMREC_SIRANO
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(RecNum)=BOMREC_INPUTTYPE
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(RecNum)=BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.BOMREC_OPERASYON(RecNum)=BOMREC_OPERASYON
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(RecNum)=BOMREC_KAYNAK0
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.BOMREC_YMAMULPS(RecNum)=BOMREC_YMAMULPS
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.BOMREC_YMAMULPM(RecNum)=BOMREC_YMAMULPM
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.HATAVAR(RecNum)=HATAVAR
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.HATAACIKLAMASI(RecNum)=HATAACIKLAMASI

	next
	
'	Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher()

	Doc.StatusbarMessage "Excel Kapatýlýyor"
	Wb.Close(false)
	Excel.Quit
End Sub
Sub AddBomInfo()	
	Dim MTable
	Dim filename
	Set MTable=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if Doc.IsBlank(MTable) then Exit Sub
	filename=Trim(MTable.FILELOKASYONU)
	if Trim(filename)="" then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Dosya Adý???","dinamo",MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	end if
	Call FromExcel(filename)	
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "FILELOKASYONU","CHAR",256,0,"L","F2=DO_SYSBRW_FILENAME()",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SECENEK","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	MTable.FILELOKASYONU="C:\ATOM\BOMBOO.xls"

	Dim BOMU01T_TEXTDATA
	Set BOMU01T_TEXTDATA=Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01T_TEXTDATA")
	if Doc.IsBlank(BOMU01T_TEXTDATA) then
'		Set BOMU01T_TEXTDATA=Doc.CreateTransactionalRowsetObject("BOMU01T_TEXTDATA=BOMU01T(NOFILE)","TMPCOPY")	
		Set BOMU01T_TEXTDATA=Doc.CreateTransactionalRowsetObject("BOMU01T_TEXTDATA=BOMU01T(NOFILE)","IO")	
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.Addfield2 "ACIKLAMA","CHAR",70,0,"L","",""
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.Addfield2 "HATAVAR","CHAR",1,0,"L","|AP10=",""
		BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.Addfield2 "HATAACIKLAMASI","CHAR",1000,0,"L","",""
	end if

	BOMU01T_TEXTDATA.Empty()

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub ApplyBomInfo()
	Dim i
	Dim j
	Dim RecNum
	Dim RetCode
	Dim EVRAKNO
	Dim OnSuccedRecordCount

	RetCode=Doc.d7MsgBox("Uygulamak Ýstediðinize Emin misiniz?","Freedom",MB_YESNO)
	if RetCode<>vbYes then
		Exit Sub
	end if

	Dim BOMU01E
	Set BOMU01E=Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01E")

	Dim BOMU01T
	Set BOMU01T=Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01T")

	Dim BOMU01T_TEXTDATA
	Set BOMU01T_TEXTDATA=Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01T_TEXTDATA")

	Dim BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_G
	Set BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_G=Doc.Select2G("_BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_123RRTYY","BOMU01T_TEXTDATA","EVRAKNO>' '","","EVRAKNO",-1)

	Dim BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D
	for i=1 to BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_G.GetRecCount()
		EVRAKNO=BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_G.EVRAKNO(i)
		Set BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D=Doc.Select2("_BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_12322222","BOMU01T_TEXTDATA","EVRAKNO='"+EVRAKNO+"' & HATAVAR='1'","",-1)
		if BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D.GetRecCount()=0 then
			Set BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D=Doc.Select2("_BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_12322222","BOMU01T_TEXTDATA","EVRAKNO='"+EVRAKNO+"'","",-1)
			if BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D.GetRecCount()>0 then
				RetCode=Doc.Load_Voucher(EVRAKNO)
				if RetCode=0 then
					BOMU01E.AKTIF_PASIF=" "
					BOMU01E.MAMULCODE=" "
					BOMU01E.MAMULMIKTAR=0
					BOMU01E.MAMULCODE_STOK00_IUNIT=" "
					BOMU01E.ACIKLAMA=" "
					BOMU01E.GK_1=" "
					BOMU01E.GK_2=" "
					BOMU01E.GK_3=" "

					BOMU01T.Empty()

					BOMU01E.AKTIF_PASIF=BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D.AKTIF_PASIF(1)
					BOMU01E.MAMULCODE=BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D.BOMREC_CODE(1)
					BOMU01E.MAMULMIKTAR=BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D.BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR(1)
					BOMU01E.ACIKLAMA=BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D.ACIKLAMA(1)

					for j=1 to BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D.GetRecCount()
						RecNum=BOMU01T.AddRow()
						BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow RecNum
						BOMU01T.BOMREC_SIRANO(RecNum)=BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D.BOMREC_SIRANO(j)
						BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(RecNum)=BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(j)
						BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(RecNum)=BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j)
						BOMU01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON(RecNum)=BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D.BOMREC_OPERASYON(j)
						BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(RecNum)=BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(j)
						BOMU01T.BOMREC_YMAMULPS(RecNum)=BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D.BOMREC_YMAMULPS(j)
						BOMU01T.BOMREC_YMAMULPM(RecNum)=BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D.BOMREC_YMAMULPM(j)
						BOMU01T.BOMREC_ALTKODU(RecNum)=BOMU01T_TEXTDATA_D.BOMREC_ALTKODU(j)
					next
					RetCode=Doc.Save_Voucher()
					if RetCode<>0 then
						Doc.d7MsgBox Trim(EVRAKNO)+" Nolu Föy Kaydedilemedi","Freedom",MB_OK
					end if
					OnSuccedRecordCount=OnSuccedRecordCount+1
				end if
				Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
			end if
		end if
	next

	if OnSuccedRecordCount>0 then
		Doc.d7MsgBox OnSuccedRecordCount & "Adet Föy Kaydedildi","Freedom",MB_OK
	else
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Deðiþiklik Yapýlmadý","Freedom",MB_OK
	end if

End Sub
Sub GO1()
	Call AddBomInfo()
'	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
Sub GO2()
	Call ApplyBomInfo()
'	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub

		//EDITTEXT D="KAYNAK_BIRIM" CAPTION= "Birim"

		COMBOBOX D="BOMREC_KAYNAK0_BU" CAPTION="Tezgah Süresi Alternatif Bilgi Giriþi Birimi"

		EDITTEXT D="OPERATORTURU1" BROWSEBUTTON=1

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AT2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ETIKET
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Etiket Kartlarýný Yarat - Silmeyin !!!!!!!!
Kod : 
Function GetEtiketTable(EtiketFormu, SatirTipi)
	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK20" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK20_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu, SatirTipi)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK68" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK68_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "SAYM01" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = SAYM01_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK60_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK25" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK25_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = TRACK25_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29" or Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29I" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK29_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK36" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK36_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK19" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK19_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK26" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = Transfer_EtiketYarat(STOK26T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK63" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = Transfer_EtiketYarat(STOK63T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK61" Then
 		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK61_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK24" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK24_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "MMPS10" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = MMPS10_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK46" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK46_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "SFDC32" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = SFDC32_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu, Miktar)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACKIZ" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = TRACKIZ_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "D7KIDSLB" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = D7KIDSLB_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	End if
End Function
Function MMPS10_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")
	
	Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set M10T = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
	M10TM.SafePack()
	For j = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		Miktar = M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(j)
		'DefKutuIciMiktar = GetStdKoliAdet(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j), M10TM.MUSTERIKODU(j), "")
		KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "KOLI_MIK",0)
		KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,0)
		LotNo = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "LOTNO",0)

		'EtkTarih = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "ETK_TRH",0)

		'if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = DefKutuIciMiktar
		'if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = Miktar
		EtiketAdedi = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "ETIK_ADT",0)	
		if EtiketAdedi = 0 Then EtiketAdedi = 1

		for i = 1 to EtiketAdedi
			Redim IDArray(1,0)
			KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar

			BarkodNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
			Doc1.Load_Voucher BarkodNo

			d7LB.SKOD = M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j)
			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = LotNo
			d7LB.SERINO = trim(BarkodNo)
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = KutuMiktari
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = M10TM.SF_SF_UNIT(j)
			d7LB.MPSNO = M10E.EVRAKNO
			d7LB.FOKOD = M10TM.FOKOD_MAMULKODU(j)
			d7LB.MPSTM_TRNUM = M10TM.TRNUM(j)

			d7LB.REFTARIH1 = Doc.BuGun()

			CHKodu = M10TM.MUSTERIKODU(j)
			if trim(CHKodu) = "" Then
				CHKodu = GetMusteriCHKodu(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j))
			End if
			d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = CHKodu
			d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
			d7LB.STATU = ""
			d7LB.APPKODU = "MMPS10"
			d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "UP"
			d7LB.EVRAKNO = M10E.EVRAKNO
			d7LB.GK_1 = "STOK"
			d7LB.REFTEXT02 = GetSonOp(M10T)
			SonIs = GetSonIs(M10T)
			d7LB.REFTEXT03 = GetSonrakiOp(M10T, SonIs)
			Doc1.Save_Voucher
			Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
		Next
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Set MMPS10_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function
Function TRACK25_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set STC = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK")
	Set S25T = Doc.Select1("de34frt5454xx", "STOK25T", "EVRAKNO='" & STC.SON_EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
	Doc.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")

	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		if (S25T.KOD(i)) <> "" Then
			Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("de444_dd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & S25T.D_ID(i) & "'", "", -1)
			if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB)
			EskiEtiket = D7LB.ILKETIKET
		End if
	Next
	if trim(EskiEtiket) <> "" Then
		Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("de444_dd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & EskiEtiket & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB)
	End if

	Set TRACK25_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function
Function STOK25_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set S25E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	S25T.SafePack()

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")

	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		SeriNo = S25T.NEWSERINO(i)
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(SeriNo), 30)
		Set D7LB_111 = Doc.Select1("dee3ffghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
		if trim(Barkod) <> "" and D7LB_111.GetRowCount = 0 Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher SeriNo
	
			d7LB.SKOD = S25T.NEWCODE(i)
			d7LB.APPKODU = "STOK25"
			d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
			d7LB.EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = S25T.NEWLOTNUMBER(i)
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = S25T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT(i)
			d7LB.VRI_NUM1 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
			d7LB.VRI_NUM2 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
			d7LB.FOKOD = S25T.NEWFOKOD(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_C = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R1 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R2 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R3 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)

			OncekiBarkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T.SERINO(i)), 30)
			Set D7LB_Eski = Doc.Select1("dee3ff44ghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & OncekiBarkod & "'", "", -1)

			if D7LB_Eski.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				d7LB.ONCEKETIKET = OncekiBarkod
				d7LB.ILKETIKET = OncekiBarkod
				d7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI = D7LB_Eski.CH_TEDARIKCI
				d7LB.SIPARISNO_46 = D7LB_Eski.SIPARISNO_46
				d7LB.SIPARTNO_46 = D7LB_Eski.SIPARTNO_46
				d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = D7LB_Eski.CH_MUSTERI
				d7LB.FORMKODU = D7LB_Eski.FORMKODU
				d7LB.STATU = D7LB_Eski.STATU
			End if
			d7LB.AMBCODE1 = S25E.TARGETAMBCODE
			
			Doc1.Save_Voucher

			Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
		Else
			Set S01 = Doc.Select1("csdc4effg", "STOK01", "SERINO='" & trim(D7LB_111.KOD(1)) & "'", "", -1)
			if D7LB_111.SF_BAKIYE <> S01.SF_MIKTAR(1) Then
				Doc1.Load_Voucher D7LB_111.KOD(1)
				d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(1) + d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK
				Doc1.Save_Voucher
				Set D7LB_111 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			End if
			d7LB1.AppendTable(D7LB_111)
		End if
	Next
	if Doc.GetDocType0 <> "TRACK251" Then
		Set S25T1 = Doc.Select2("4r4rt676hh", "STOK25T", "SERINO<>'" & Space(20) & "'", "", -1)
		S25T1.GroupBy("SERINO")
		For i = 1 to S25T1.GetRowCount()
			OncekiBarkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T1.SERINO(i)), 30)
			Set D7LB_Eski = Doc.Select1("dee3ff44ghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & OncekiBarkod & "'", "", -1)
			if D7LB_Eski.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				Set S01 = Doc.Select1("csdc4effg", "STOK01", "SERINO='" & trim(D7LB_Eski.KOD(1)) & "'", "", -1)
				if S01.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
					D7LB_Eski.SF_BAKIYE = S01.SF_MIKTAR(1)
					d7LB1.AppendTable(D7LB_Eski)
				End if
			End if
		Next
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Set STOK25_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function
Function STOK20_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu, SatirTipi)
'	Set S20T = Doc.Select2("ssw3___xs", "STOK20T", "SERINO<>'" & Space(20) & "'", "", -1)
'	S20T.GroupBy2("SERINO")
	Set S20E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Set S20T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")

	satirNo = S20T.GetCurrentRow()
	if trim(S20T.SERINO) = "" Then S20T.SERINO = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
	EtiketBasilmasin = False
	if SatirTipi = 1 Then
		baslangicSatir = satirNo
		bitisSatir = satirNo
	Else
		baslangicSatir = 1
		bitisSatir = S20T.GetRowCount()
	End if
	For i = baslangicSatir to bitisSatir
		if Left(S20T.KOD(i), 1) = "H" Then
			if trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)) = "" Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Lot No boþ býrakýlamaz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
				EtiketBasilmasin = True
			Else
				Set S31E = Doc.Select1("ddf45t677h", "SFDC31E", "LOTNUMBER='" & S20T.LOTNUMBER(i) & "'", "", -1)
				if S31E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
					Doc.d7MsgBox "Lot No : " & trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)) & " için operasyon bildirimi gerçekleþmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
					EtiketBasilmasin = True
				Else
					if trim(S31E.STOK_CODE) <> trim(S20T.KOD(i)) Then
						Doc.d7MsgBox "Lot No : " & trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)) & " baþka stok koduna ait", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
						EtiketBasilmasin = True
					Elseif trim(S31E.MPSNO) <> trim(S20T.MPSNO(i)) Then
						Doc.d7MsgBox "Lot No : " & trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)) & " baþka MPS 'e ait", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
						EtiketBasilmasin = True
					End if
				End if
			End if
		End if
		if Not EtiketBasilmasin Then
			Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S20T.SERINO(i)), 30)
			Set D7LB_111 = Doc.Select1("dee3ffghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
'			if trim(Barkod) <> "" and D7LB_111.GetRowCount = 0 Then
			if trim(Barkod) <> "" Then
				Doc1.Load_Voucher Barkod
	
				d7LB.SKOD = S20T.KOD(i)
				d7LB.APPKODU = "STOK20"
				d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "MG"
				d7LB.EVRAKNO = S20E.EVRAKNO
				d7LB.TRNUM = S20T.TRNUM(i)
				d7LB.LOTNUMBER = S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)
				d7LB.SERINO = S20T.SERINO(i)
				d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = S20T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(i)
				d7LB.SF_UNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", S20T.KOD(i), "")
				d7LB.MPSNO = S20T.MPSNO(i)
				d7LB.MPSTM_TRNUM = S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
				d7LB.VRI_NUM1 = S20T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
				d7LB.VRI_NUM2 = S20T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
				d7LB.FOKOD = S20T.FOKOD(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_C = S20T.SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_R1 = S20T.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_R2 = S20T.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_R3 = S20T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)
				d7LB.AMBCODE1 = S20E.AMBCODE
				d7LB.LOCATION1 = S20T.LOCATION1(i)
				d7LB.LOCATION2 = S20T.LOCATION2(i)
				d7LB.LOCATION3 = S20T.LOCATION3(i)
				d7LB.LOCATION4 = S20T.LOCATION4(i)
'				d7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI = S20E.CARIHESAPCODE
				'd7LB.SIPARISNO_46 = S20T.SIPNO(i)
				'd7LB.SIPARTNO_46 = S20T.SIPARTNO(i)
				d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
				d7LB.REFTARIH1 = S20E.TARIH
'				d7LB.REFTEXT01 = S20E.XIRSALIYENO
				d7LB.STATU = "OK"
				Doc1.Save_Voucher

				Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
				d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
	'		Else
	'			d7LB1.AppendTable(D7LB_111)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Set STOK20_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function

Function TRACKIZ_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Doc.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")
	Set d7LB  = Doc.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
	Set d7ST = Doc.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB_ST")
	Set STCK = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_ST")
	Set TRACKIZ_EtiketYarat = d7LB1

	IsNo = d7ST.JOBNO
	if trim(IsNo) = "" or trim(d7ST.ISMERKEZI) = "" Then 
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþ No ve Tezgah barkodlarýný okutmalýsýnýz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
	KutuIciMiktar = InputBox("Kasa Ýçi Adedi Giriniz")
	KutuIciMiktar = Doc.CDbl(trim(KutuIciMiktar))

	if KutuIciMiktar = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("dde4434f556", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='" & Left(IsNo, 10) & "'", "", -1)
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("dde4434f556MM", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & Left(IsNo, 10) & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "", -1)
	For j = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		LotNo = Mid(Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/", ""), 3) & "-" & trim(M10TM.EVRAKNO)
		EtiketAdedi = 1

		for i = 1 to EtiketAdedi
			KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar

			BarkodNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
			Doc.Load_Voucher BarkodNo

			d7LB.SKOD = M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j)
			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = LotNo
			d7LB.SERINO = trim(BarkodNo)
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = KutuMiktari
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = M10TM.SF_SF_UNIT(j)
			d7LB.MPSNO = M10TM.EVRAKNO(j)
			d7LB.FOKOD = M10TM.FOKOD_MAMULKODU(j)
			d7LB.MPSTM_TRNUM = M10TM.TRNUM(j)

			d7LB.REFTARIH1 = Doc.BuGun()

			CHKodu = M10TM.MUSTERIKODU(j)
			if trim(CHKodu) = "" Then
				CHKodu = GetMusteriCHKodu(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j))
			End if
			d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = CHKodu
			d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
			d7LB.STATU = "OK"
			d7LB.APPKODU = "MMPS10"
			d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "UP"
			d7LB.EVRAKNO = M10TM.EVRAKNO
			SonIs = GetSonIs(M10T)
			if trim(d7ST.JOBNO) = trim(SonIs) Then d7LB.GK_1 = "STOK"
			d7LB.REFTEXT02 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_OPERASYON", d7ST.JOBNO, "JOBNO")
			d7LB.REFTEXT03 = GetSonrakiOp(M10T, d7ST.JOBNO)
			Doc.Save_Voucher
			Set d7LB2 = Doc.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
		Next
	Next
	Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Set TRACKIZ_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
	d7LB1.SafePack()
	STCK.D_ID = d7LB1.SERINO
	if trim(IsNo) = trim(SonIs) Then Call UretimFisiOlustur(Doc1, d7LB1)
End Function

	Set D7KIDSLB_EtiketYarat = Doc.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
End Function

Function GetFasonInfo(Kod, LotNo, SeriNo)
	Dim FasonArr(2)
	QStr = "KOD='" & Kod & "'&LOTNUMBER='" & LotNo & "'&SERINO='" & SeriNo & "'"
	Set S68T = Doc.Select1("deefft66", "STOK68T", QStr, "", -1)
	if S68T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		FasonArr(0) = S68T.CHCODE
		FasonArr(1) = S68T.SIPNO
		FasonArr(2) = S68T.SIPARTNO
	End if
	GetFasonInfo = FasonArr
End Function
Function GetMusteriCHKodu(StokKodu)
	Set S0GT = Doc.Select1("d3r4gh77u", "STOK0GT", "STOKCODE='" & StokKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if S0GT.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		GetMusteriCHKodu = S0GT.KOD
	End if
End Function
Function GetSonrakiOp(M10TX, IsNo)
	SrNum = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", IsNo, "JOBNO")
	Set M10T1 = Doc.Select2("ded44hh7jx", M10TX.GetAppName, "R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM>'" & SrNum & "'", "SRNUM", -1)
	if M10T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetSonrakiOp = M10T1.R_OPERASYON(1)
	Else
		GetSonrakiOp = GetSonrakiUrunIlkOp(M10TX.R_MAMULKODU)
	End if
End Function
Function GetSonrakiUrunIlkOp(StokKodu)
	Set B01T = Doc.Select1("de3333r5t", "BOMU01T", "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'&BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='" & StokKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if B01T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Set B01T1 = Doc.Select1("d44f5", "BOMU01T", "EVRAKNO='" & B01T.EVRAKNO & "'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='I'", "SRNUM", 1)
		if B01T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			GetSonrakiUrunIlkOp = B01T1.BOMREC_OPERASYON
		End if
	End if
End Function
Function GetSonOp(M10T)
	Set M10TI = Doc.Select2("de444fr5ttt", M10T.GetAppName, "R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "<SRNUM", -1)
	if M10TI.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetSonOp = M10TI.R_OPERASYON
	End if
End Function
Function GetSonIs(M10T)
	Set M10TI = Doc.Select2("de444fr5ttt", M10T.GetAppName, "R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "<SRNUM", -1)
	if M10TI.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetSonIs = M10TI.JOBNO
	End if
End Function
Sub UretimFisiOlustur(Doc1, d7LB1)
	Set S20E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Set S20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20T")

	Set S20T1 = Doc.Select1("dd33f56jjj77", "STOK20T", "MPSNO='" & d7LB1.MPSNO & "'&TARIH='" & Doc.BuGun & "'", "", -1)
	if S20T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		if Doc1.Load_Voucher(S20T1.EVRAKNO) <> 0 Then
			Doc1.New_Voucher
			if Left(D7LB1.SKOD, 1) = "3" Then
				S20E.AMBCODE = "MA"
			Else
				S20E.AMBCODE = "IM"
			End if
			S20E.IMALATAMBCODE = "IM"
			S20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "IM"
		End if
	Else
		Doc1.New_Voucher
		if Left(D7LB1.SKOD, 1) = "3" Then
			S20E.AMBCODE = "MA"
		Else
			S20E.AMBCODE = "IM"
		End if
		S20E.IMALATAMBCODE = "IM"
		S20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "IM"
	End if		

	For i = 1 to d7LB1.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S20T.AddRow()
		S20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S20T.MPSNO = d7LB1.MPSNO(i)
		if trim(S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM) = "" Then S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM = d7LB1.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
		S20T.LOTNUMBER = d7LB1.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S20T.SERINO = d7LB1.SERINO(i)
		S20T.FOKOD = d7LB1.FOKOD(i)
		S20T.SF_MIKTAR = D7LB1.SF_MIKTAR(i)
	Next
	Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_003"
	Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_STOK01DEBULUNDUMU_BILGISATIRIE"

	Doc1.Save_Voucher()

	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set S00 = Doc.Select1("ssss", "STOK00", "TSENO>'.'", "", -1)	
	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc.Load_Voucher S00.KOD(i)
		Call KTA_Yaz(S00, i)
		STOK00.TEXT_TECH_1 = ""
		STOK00.TEXT_TECH_2 = ""
		STOK00.TEXT_TECH_3 = ""
		STOK00.TSENO = ""
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	Next
End Sub
Sub KTA_Yaz(S00, i)
	Dim T_STOK00    
	Set T_STOK00 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")   
	Dim nret    
	nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001","STOK00",S00.KOD(i))   
	Dim T_U1    
	Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")   
	K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("GERILIM",-1, S00.TEXT_TECH_1(i))   
	K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("KESIT",-1,S00.TEXT_TECH_2(i))   
	K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("STANDARD",-1,S00.TEXT_TECH_3(i))   
	K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("KABLO_TUR",-1,S00.TSENO(i))   
	T_U1.Exec "SAVE"   
End Sub

Sub IseGirisBilgileriDoldur(ByVal ExcelSheet,ByVal Satir,ByVal PersonelNo)
	Dim Tbl_PERSMBT5
	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_CALISMA_UCRET_YENI_GIRIS_BASLA"
	Set Tbl_PERSMBT5=Doc.GetTableObject("PERSM0BT5")

	Dim AlanIsmi
	Dim AlanDegeri
	Dim Sutun
	For Sutun=2 To 100
		AlanIsmi=Trim(ExcelSheet.Cells(3, Sutun))
		AlanDegeri=ExcelSheet.Cells(Satir, Sutun)
		If AlanIsmi<>"" Then
			Tbl_PERSMBT5.SetFieldValue AlanIsmi,0,AlanDegeri
		End If
	Next

	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_CALISMA_UCRET_YENI_GIRIS_OK"
End Sub


Sub Makro1()
	Dim myExcel
	Dim mySheet
	Set myExcel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")

	myExcel.Workbooks.Open ("C:\CALISMA.XLS")
	Set mySheet = myExcel.Workbooks("CALISMA.XLS").Worksheets("CALISMA")

	Dim PersonelNo

	Dim I		
	For I=4 To 1000
		PersonelNo=Trim(mySheet.Cells(I, 1))
		If PersonelNo<>"" Then
			PersonelNo=Doc.LeftJustify(PersonelNo,16)
			Doc.Load_Voucher PersonelNo
			If Trim(Doc.GetLoadedVoucher())=Trim(PersonelNo) Then
				Call IseGirisBilgileriDoldur(mySheet,I,PersonelNo)
				Doc.Save_Voucher
			Else
				MsgBox(Trim(PersonelNo) & " Nolu Personel Bulunamadý")
			End If
		End If
	Next

	myExcel.Quit

	Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable = Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "YENI","CHAR",15,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "ESKI","CHAR",15,0,"L","",""

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub MetrajlariGuncelle()
	Set MTable=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	YeniMetraj = trim(MTable.YENI)
	EskiMetraj = trim(MTable.ESKI)
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL

	if not isObject(STACK) Then
		Set STACK = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK")
	End if
	if trim(STACK.SON_EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		Set S25T = Doc.Select1("STOK25TPR","STOK25T", "EVRAKNO='"+trim(STACK.SON_EVRAKNO)+"'", "", -1)
		Set S25E = Doc.Select1("STOK25EPR","STOK25E", "EVRAKNO='"+trim(STACK.SON_EVRAKNO)+"'", "", -1)
		if S25T.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
	End if
	EskiSeriNo = S25T.SERINO(1)
	YeniSeriNo = S25T.NEWSERINO(1)

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.Load_Voucher EskiSeriNo
	d7LB.VRI_VR_R1 = EskiMetraj
	Doc1.Save_Voucher

	Doc1.Load_Voucher YeniSeriNo
	d7LB.VRI_VR_R1 = YeniMetraj
	Doc1.Save_Voucher

	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc.DeleteDocumentNoUI "D7KIDSLB", ""
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             

Ekran Formu

FORM MAKROFORMU1 HEIGHT=140P WIDTH=230P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS
SET CAPTION="dinamo"

MENUDEF
	POPUP TEXT=" & Dosya"
	MENUITEM TEXT="Ýþlem Yapmadan Çý & kýþ" ID=ID_APP_CANCEL KEY=ESCAPE
	ENDPOPUP
ENDMENUDEF

LOCATE TOP=0
LTEXT W=25 LEFT=3p TOP=+5p T="Makaralar Ýçin Metrajlarý Giriniz(Alt-Üst) :"
RTEXT W=10 LEFT=3p TOP=+1 T="Yeni Makara :"
EDITTEXT LEFT=E+1 TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE.YENI

BUTTON LEFT=9 TOP=+30p WIDTH=10 HEIGHT=30p CAPTION="Tamam" ID="MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','MetrajlariGuncelle')"

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AT2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MPSHESAPLA
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : MPS 'lerin tezgah sürelerini yeniden hesapla
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to LISTET.GetRowCount()
		Doc.Load_Voucher LISTET.EVRAKNO(i)
		Call MPSGuncelle()
	Next
End Sub
Sub MPSGuncelle()
	HesapOpt = MMPS10E.FIXEDRECETE
	QStr = "R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_KAYNAKKODU>='H38'&R_KAYNAKKODU<='H39'"
	Set M10T = Doc.Select2("dde", "MMPS10T", QStr, "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub

	MMPS10E.FIXEDRECETE = ""
	Doc.Save_Voucher()
	For i = 1 to MMPS10T.GetRowCount()
		if Left(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), 3) = "H38" Then
			MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i) = ""
			MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) = ""
		End if
	Next
	Alanlar = MMPS10T.GetFieldNames("N")
	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MMPS10T.AddRow
		MMPS10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		For each alan in Alanlar
			Deger = M10T.GetFieldValue(Alan,i)
			MMPS10T.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, Deger
		Next
	Next
	MMPS10T.Sort("R_KAYNAKTYPE+R_SIRANO")
	MMPS10E.FIXEDRECETE = HesapOpt
	Doc.Save_Voucher()
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AT2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MUH
Hangi Tablo için : TECH10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Mühendislik Tanýmlarý Toplu Güncelleme (XLS) MUHENDISLIK.XLS dosyasýndan alýyor
Kod : 
Option Explicit
Sub FromExcel(filename)
	Dim i
	Dim j
	Dim Sh
	Dim Wb
	Dim Excel

	Dim TECH10T_TEXTDATA
	Set TECH10T_TEXTDATA=Doc.GetTableObject("TECH10T_TEXTDATA")
	TECH10T_TEXTDATA.Empty()

	Doc.StatusbarMessage "Excel Açýlýyor"

	on error resume next

	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Open(filename)
	Set Sh = Wb.Sheets("TECH10")

	if Err.Number<>0 then
		Doc.d7MsgBox Err.Number & "->" & Err.Description,"freedom",MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	end if

	Doc.StatusbarMessage "Okunuyor"

	Dim DataIlkSatir
	Dim DataSonSatir
	Dim DataBitisSatiriReferansKolon

	DataIlkSatir=2
	DataBitisSatiriReferansKolon=3

	Dim vval
	for i=DataIlkSatir to 65000
		vval=Sh.Cells(i,DataBitisSatiriReferansKolon)
		if Trim(vval)="" then
			Exit For
		end if
		DataSonSatir=i
	next

	Dim col_EVRAKNO
	Dim col_KRITERCODE_1
	Dim col_KRITERCODE_2
	Dim col_KRITERCODE_3
	Dim col_KRITERCODE_4
	Dim col_KRITERCODE_5
	Dim col_KRITERCODE_6
	Dim col_KRITERCODE_7
	Dim col_KRITERCODE_8
	Dim col_KRITERCODE_9
	Dim col_KRITERCODE_10
	Dim col_KRITERVALUE_1
	Dim col_KRITERVALUE_2
	Dim col_KRITERVALUE_3
	Dim col_KRITERVALUE_4
	Dim col_KRITERVALUE_5
	Dim col_KRITERVALUE_6
	Dim col_KRITERVALUE_7
	Dim col_KRITERVALUE_8
	Dim col_KRITERVALUE_9
	Dim col_KRITERVALUE_10
	Dim col_NOTES
	Dim col_REPLACEEVRAKNO
	Dim col_TSCODE
	Dim col_TSTEXT

	Dim EVRAKNO
	Dim KRITERCODE_1
	Dim KRITERCODE_2
	Dim KRITERCODE_3
	Dim KRITERCODE_4
	Dim KRITERCODE_5
	Dim KRITERCODE_6
	Dim KRITERCODE_7
	Dim KRITERCODE_8
	Dim KRITERCODE_9
	Dim KRITERCODE_10
	Dim KRITERVALUE_1
	Dim KRITERVALUE_2
	Dim KRITERVALUE_3
	Dim KRITERVALUE_4
	Dim KRITERVALUE_5
	Dim KRITERVALUE_6
	Dim KRITERVALUE_7
	Dim KRITERVALUE_8
	Dim KRITERVALUE_9
	Dim KRITERVALUE_10
	Dim NOTES
	Dim REPLACEEVRAKNO
	Dim TSCODE
	Dim TSTEXT
	Dim HATAVAR
	Dim HATAACIKLAMASI

	col_EVRAKNO=3
	col_KRITERCODE_1=12
	col_KRITERCODE_2=14
'	col_KRITERCODE_3
'	col_KRITERCODE_4
'	col_KRITERCODE_5
'	col_KRITERCODE_6
'	col_KRITERCODE_7
'	col_KRITERCODE_8
'	col_KRITERCODE_9
'	col_KRITERCODE_10
	col_KRITERVALUE_1=13
	col_KRITERVALUE_2=15
'	col_KRITERVALUE_3
'	col_KRITERVALUE_4
'	col_KRITERVALUE_5
'	col_KRITERVALUE_6
'	col_KRITERVALUE_7
'	col_KRITERVALUE_8
'	col_KRITERVALUE_9
'	col_KRITERVALUE_10
	col_NOTES=10
'	col_REPLACEEVRAKNO
'	col_TSCODE=4
	col_TSTEXT=11

	Dim RecNum
	Dim dummyVal

	for i=DataIlkSatir to DataSonSatir
		HATAVAR="0"
		HATAACIKLAMASI=""
		EVRAKNO=Sh.Cells(i,col_EVRAKNO)
		KRITERCODE_1=Sh.Cells(i,col_KRITERCODE_1)
		KRITERCODE_2=Sh.Cells(i,col_KRITERCODE_2)
		KRITERVALUE_1=Sh.Cells(i,col_KRITERVALUE_1)
		KRITERVALUE_2=Sh.Cells(i,col_KRITERVALUE_2)
		NOTES=Sh.Cells(i,col_NOTES)
		TSTEXT=Sh.Cells(i,col_TSTEXT)

		EVRAKNO=Doc.RightJustify(EVRAKNO,10)
		if Trim(EVRAKNO)="" then
			HATAVAR="1"
			HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"[Föy No Boþ]"
		end if

		dummyVal=Doc.GetGecoValue("|TCHCK","KOD",KRITERCODE_1,0)		
		if Trim(dummyVal)="" then
			HATAVAR="1"
			HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"["+Trim(KRITERCODE_1)+"] Tanýmlý Deðil"
		end if

		dummyVal=KRITERVALUE_1
		if Trim(dummyVal)="" then
			HATAVAR="1"
			HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"[1. Kriter Kodu Tanýmlý. Kriter Boþ]"
		end if

		dummyVal=Doc.GetGecoValue("|TCHCK","KOD",KRITERCODE_2,0)		
		if Trim(dummyVal)="" then
			HATAVAR="1"
			HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"["+Trim(KRITERCODE_2)+"] Tanýmlý Deðil"
		end if

		dummyVal=KRITERVALUE_2
		if Trim(dummyVal)="" then
			HATAVAR="1"
			HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"[2. Kriter Kodu Tanýmlý. Kriter Boþ]"
		end if		

		dummyVal=TSTEXT
		if Trim(dummyVal)="" then
			HATAVAR="1"
			HATAACIKLAMASI=HATAACIKLAMASI+"[Taným Boþ]"
		end if			

		RecNum=TECH10T_TEXTDATA.AddRow()
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.EVRAKNO(RecNum)=EVRAKNO
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.KRITERCODE_1(RecNum)=KRITERCODE_1
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.KRITERCODE_2(RecNum)=KRITERCODE_2
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.KRITERVALUE_1(RecNum)=KRITERVALUE_1
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.KRITERVALUE_2(RecNum)=KRITERVALUE_2
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.NOTES(RecNum)=NOTES
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.TSTEXT(RecNum)=TSTEXT
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.HATAVAR(RecNum)=HATAVAR
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.HATAACIKLAMASI(RecNum)=HATAACIKLAMASI

	next
	
'	Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher()

	Doc.StatusbarMessage "Excel Kapatýlýyor"
	Wb.Close(false)
	Excel.Quit
End Sub
Sub AddTEchInfo()	
	Dim MTable
	Dim filename
	Set MTable=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if Doc.IsBlank(MTable) then Exit Sub
	filename=Trim(MTable.FILELOKASYONU)
	if Trim(filename)="" then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Dosya Adý???","dinamo",MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	end if
	Call FromExcel(filename)	
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "FILELOKASYONU","CHAR",256,0,"L","F2=DO_SYSBRW_FILENAME()",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "PARABIRIMLERI","CHAR",4,0,"L","","*PUNIT"
	MTable.Addfield2 "SECENEK","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	MTable.FILELOKASYONU="C:\ATOM\MUHENDISLIK.xls"

	Dim TECH10T_TEXTDATA
	Set TECH10T_TEXTDATA=Doc.GetTableObject("TECH10T_TEXTDATA")
	if Doc.IsBlank(TECH10T_TEXTDATA) then
'		Set TECH10T_TEXTDATA=Doc.CreateTransactionalRowsetObject("TECH10T_TEXTDATA=TECH10T(NOFILE)","TMPCOPY")	
		Set TECH10T_TEXTDATA=Doc.CreateTransactionalRowsetObject("TECH10T_TEXTDATA=TECH10T(NOFILE)","IO")	
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.Addfield2 "KRITERCODE_1","CHAR",4,0,"L","",""

		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.Addfield2 "KRITERCODE_4","CHAR",4,0,"L","",""
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.Addfield2 "KRITERCODE_5","CHAR",4,0,"L","",""
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.Addfield2 "KRITERCODE_6","CHAR",4,0,"L","",""
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.Addfield2 "KRITERCODE_7","CHAR",4,0,"L","",""
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.Addfield2 "KRITERCODE_8","CHAR",4,0,"L","",""
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.Addfield2 "KRITERCODE_9","CHAR",4,0,"L","",""

		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.Addfield2 "KRITERVALUE_2","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.Addfield2 "KRITERVALUE_3","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""

		TECH10T_TEXTDATA.Addfield2 "KRITERVALUE_8","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""

	TECH10T_TEXTDATA.Empty()

	Dim Par

	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par

Sub ApplyTechInfo()
	Dim i
	Dim j
	Dim RecNum
	Dim RetCode
	Dim EVRAKNO
	Dim OnSuccedRecordCount

	RetCode=Doc.d7MsgBox("Uygulamak Ýstediðinize Emin misiniz?","Freedom",MB_YESNO)
	if RetCode<>vbYes then
		Exit Sub
	end if

	Dim TECH10E
	Set TECH10E=Doc.GetTableObject("TECH10E")

	Dim TECH10T
	Set TECH10T=Doc.GetTableObject("TECH10T")

	Dim TECH10T_TEXTDATA
	Set TECH10T_TEXTDATA=Doc.GetTableObject("TECH10T_TEXTDATA")

	Dim TECH10T_TEXTDATA_G
	Set TECH10T_TEXTDATA_G=Doc.Select2G("_TECH10T_TEXTDATA_123RRTYY","TECH10T_TEXTDATA","EVRAKNO>' '","","EVRAKNO",-1)

	Dim TECH10T_TEXTDATA_D
	for i=1 to TECH10T_TEXTDATA_G.GetRecCount()
		EVRAKNO=TECH10T_TEXTDATA_G.EVRAKNO(i)
		Set TECH10T_TEXTDATA_D=Doc.Select2("_TECH10T_TEXTDATA_12322222","TECH10T_TEXTDATA","EVRAKNO='"+EVRAKNO+"' & HATAVAR='1'","",-1)
		if TECH10T_TEXTDATA_D.GetRecCount()=0 then
			Set TECH10T_TEXTDATA_D=Doc.Select2("_TECH10T_TEXTDATA_12322222","TECH10T_TEXTDATA","EVRAKNO='"+EVRAKNO+"'","",-1)
			if TECH10T_TEXTDATA_D.GetRecCount()>0 then
				RetCode=Doc.Load_Voucher(EVRAKNO)
				if RetCode=0 then
					TECH10E.KRITERCODE_1=""
					TECH10E.KRITERCODE_2=""
					TECH10E.KRITERCODE_3=""
					TECH10E.KRITERCODE_4=""
					TECH10E.KRITERCODE_5=""
					TECH10E.KRITERCODE_6=""
					TECH10E.KRITERCODE_7=""
					TECH10E.KRITERCODE_8=""
					TECH10E.KRITERCODE_9=""
					TECH10E.KRITERCODE_10=""

					TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_1=""
					TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_2=""
					TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_3=""
					TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_4=""
					TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_5=""
					TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_6=""
					TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_7=""
					TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_8=""
					TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_9=""
					TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_10=""
					TECH10E.NOTES=""

					TECH10T.Empty()

					TECH10E.NOTES=TECH10T_TEXTDATA_D.NOTES(1)

					TECH10E.KRITERCODE_1=TECH10T_TEXTDATA_D.KRITERCODE_1(1)
					TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_1=TECH10T_TEXTDATA_D.KRITERVALUE_1(1)

					TECH10E.KRITERCODE_2=TECH10T_TEXTDATA_D.KRITERCODE_2(1)
					TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_2=TECH10T_TEXTDATA_D.KRITERVALUE_2(1)

					for j=1 to TECH10T_TEXTDATA_D.GetRecCount()
						RecNum=TECH10T.AddRow()
						TECH10T.SetCurrentRow RecNum
						TECH10T.TSTEXT(RecNum)=TECH10T_TEXTDATA_D.TSTEXT(j)
					next

					RetCode=Doc.Save_Voucher()
					if RetCode<>0 then
						Doc.d7MsgBox Trim(EVRAKNO)+" Nolu Föy Kaydedilemedi","Freedom",MB_OK
					end if
					OnSuccedRecordCount=OnSuccedRecordCount+1
				end if
				Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
			end if
		end if
	next

	if OnSuccedRecordCount>0 then
		Doc.d7MsgBox OnSuccedRecordCount & "Adet Föy Kaydedildi","Freedom",MB_OK
	else
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Deðiþiklik Yapýlmadý","Freedom",MB_OK
	end if

End Sub
Sub GO1()
	Call AddTEchInfo()
'	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
Sub GO2()
	Call ApplyTechInfo()
'	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AT2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MUHTZIRH
Hangi Tablo için : TECH10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Mühendislik Tanýmlarý 'ndaki Zýrh operasyon kodlarýný deðiþtir
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to 6
		QStr = "KRITERCODE_" & i & "='OKOD'&KRITERVALUE_" & i & "='010'"
		Set T10E = Doc.Select1("ddee", "TECH10E", QStr, "", -1)
		For j = 1 to T10E.GetRowCount()
			Doc.StatusBarMessage i & " of 6 - " & j & " / " & T10E.GetRowCount()
			Doc.Load_Voucher T10E.EVRAKNO(j)
			StokKodu = GetStokKodu()
			if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
				Gk9 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_9", StokKodu, "")
				YeniOp = trim(TECH10E.GetFieldValue("KRITERVALUE_" & i, 1)) & Gk9
				TECH10E.SetFieldValue "KRITERVALUE_" & i, -1, YeniOp
				Doc.Save_Voucher
			End if
		Next
	Next
	Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Function GetStokKodu()
	For i = 1 to 10
		if trim(TECH10E.GetFieldValue("KRITERCODE_" & i, 1)) = "SKOD" Then
			GetStokKodu = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(TECH10E.GetFieldValue("KRITERVALUE_" & i, 1)),24)
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("TABLO1_KRITERLER=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "B_TARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "E_TARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "BASLANGIC_SAATI","CHAR",5,0,"L","","||SY_TIME5"

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("TABLO1=STACK","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD_IMLT00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	For i = 0 to 40
		MTableT.Addfield2 "REPCOL" & i, "DOUBLE",12,1,"R","",""
	Next
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0

	Dim MTableT2
	Set MTableT2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("TABLO2=STACK","IO")
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "KOD_IMLT00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	For i = 0 to 40
		MTableT2.Addfield2 "REPCOL" & i, "DOUBLE",12,1,"R","",""
	Next
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0

	Dim MTableT3
	Set MTableT3=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("TABLO3=STACK","IO")
	MTableT3.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT3.Addfield2 "KOD_IMLT00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	For i = 0 to 40
		MTableT3.Addfield2 "REPCOL" & i, "DOUBLE",12,1,"R","",""
	Next
	MTableT3.SetRowAID 0,0,0

	Dim MTableT4
	Set MTableT4=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("TABLO4=STACK","IO")
	MTableT4.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT4.Addfield2 "KOD_IMLT00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	For i = 0 to 40
		MTableT4.Addfield2 "REPCOL" & i, "DOUBLE",12,1,"R","",""
	Next
	MTableT4.SetRowAID 0,0,0


	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "OEE",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub OEEHesapla()
	Set KriterTable = Doc.GetTableObject("TABLO1_KRITERLER")
	QStr = "RECTARIH1>='" & KriterTable.B_TARIH & "'"
	if trim(KriterTable.E_TARIH) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & "&RECTARIH2<='" & Doc.StrDate_Increment(KriterTable.E_TARIH, 1) & "'"
	End if

	BaslangicSerial = GetDateTimeSerial(KriterTable.B_TARIH, KriterTable.BASLANGIC_SAATI)
	if trim(KriterTable.E_TARIH) <> "" Then
		BitisSerial = GetDateTimeSerial(Doc.StrDate_Increment(KriterTable.E_TARIH,1), KriterTable.BASLANGIC_SAATI)
	End if

	Set T73T = Doc.Select1("der5ggt5", "TABL73T", "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&STATU='P'", "", -1)
	Set S20T = Doc.Select1("sefw4fsrtsdty", "SFDC20T", QStr, "", -1)
	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Üretim Kayýtlarý Oluþturuluyor : " & i & " / " & S20T.GetRowCount()
		S20T.NREF01(i) = GetDateTimeSerial(S20T.RECTARIH1(i), S20T.RECTIME1(i))
		S20T.NREF02(i) = GetDateTimeSerial(S20T.RECTARIH2(i), S20T.RECTIME2(i))
		if S20T.RECTIME2(i) <= KriterTable.BASLANGIC_SAATI Then
			S20T.RECTARIH1(i) = Doc.StrDate_Increment(S20T.RECTARIH1(i), -1)
		End if
		if S20T.D7_ISLEM_KODU(i) = "U" Then
			S20T.NREF03(i) = S20T.SURE(i)
		Elseif S20T.D7_ISLEM_KODU(i) = "A" Then
			S20T.NREF04(i) = S20T.SURE(i)
		Elseif S20T.D7_ISLEM_KODU(i) = "D" Then
			if PlanliDurusMu(S20T.DURMA_SEBEBI(i), T73T) Then
				S20T.NREF05(i) = S20T.SURE(i)
			Else
				S20T.NREF06(i) = S20T.SURE(i)
			End if
		End if
		S20T.NREF07(i) = GetPlanlananSure(S20T.TM_MIKTAR(i), S20T.JOBNO(i))
		S20T.NREF08(i) = GetHurdaMiktar(S20T.EVRAKNO(i), S20T.SFDC20S_OR_TRNUM(i))
	Next
	QStr1 = "NREF01>=" & BaslangicSerial
	if trim(KriterTable.E_TARIH) <> "" Then
		QStr1 = QStr1 & "&NREF02<=" & BitisSerial
	End if
	Set S20T1 = Doc.Select2G("sdfeff4tyyh", S20T.GetAppName, QStr1, "KOD+RECTARIH1", "KOD+RECTARIH1", -1)
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("TABLO1")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("TABLO2")
	Set MTableT3 = Doc.GetTableObject("TABLO3")
	Set MTableT4 = Doc.GetTableObject("TABLO4")

	MTableT1.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT1.Empty

	MTableT2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT2.Empty

	MTableT3.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT3.Empty

	MTableT4.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT4.Empty

	For i = 1 to S20T1.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "OEE Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S20T1.GetRowCount()
		if S20T1.KOD(i) <> EskiKod Then
			satirNo = MTableT1.AddRow
			MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			MTableT1.KOD = S20T1.KOD(i)
			MTableT1.KOD_IMLT00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", S20T1.KOD(i), "")

			satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow
			MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			MTableT2.KOD = S20T1.KOD(i)
			MTableT2.KOD_IMLT00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", S20T1.KOD(i), "")

			satirNo = MTableT3.AddRow
			MTableT3.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			MTableT3.KOD = S20T1.KOD(i)
			MTableT3.KOD_IMLT00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", S20T1.KOD(i), "")

			satirNo = MTableT4.AddRow
			MTableT4.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			MTableT4.KOD = S20T1.KOD(i)
			MTableT4.KOD_IMLT00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", S20T1.KOD(i), "")
		End if
		Gun = Doc.CDateToInt(S20T1.RECTARIH1(i)) - Doc.CDateToInt(KriterTable.B_TARIH) + 1
		if Gun >= 1 and Gun <= 40 Then
			PlanliDurusSuresi = 	S20T1.NREF05(i)
			PlanSizDurusSuresi = S20T1.NREF06(i)
			CalismaSuresi = S20T1.NREF03(i)
			HazirBulunmaOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(CalismaSuresi, S20T1.SURE(i) - PlanliDurusSuresi) * 100, 1)

			PlanlananSure = S20T1.NREF07(i)
			PerformansVerimliligi = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(PlanlananSure, CalismaSuresi) * 100, 1)

			HurdaMiktari = S20T1.NREF08(i)
			KalitePerformansi = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(S20T1.TM_MIKTAR(i), S20T1.TM_MIKTAR(i) + HurdaMiktari) * 100, 1)
			OEE = HazirBulunmaOrani * PerformansVerimliligi * KalitePerformansi / 10000

			MTableT1.SetFieldValue "REPCOL" & Gun, -1, OEE
			MTableT2.SetFieldValue "REPCOL" & Gun, -1, PerformansVerimliligi
			MTableT3.SetFieldValue "REPCOL" & Gun, -1, HazirBulunmaOrani
			MTableT4.SetFieldValue "REPCOL" & Gun, -1, KalitePerformansi
		End if

		EskiKod = S20T1.KOD(i)
	Next

	S20T1.GroupBy("KOD")
	For i = 1 to S20T1.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Dönem Toplamlarý Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S20T1.GetRowCount()
		For j = 1 to MTableT1.GetRowCount()
			if trim(MTableT1.KOD(j)) = trim(S20T1.KOD(i)) Then
				satirNo = i
				Exit For
			End if
		Next
		MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT3.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT4.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		PlanliDurusSuresi = 	S20T1.NREF05(i)
		PlanSizDurusSuresi = S20T1.NREF06(i)
		CalismaSuresi = S20T1.NREF03(i)
		HazirBulunmaOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(CalismaSuresi, S20T1.SURE(i) - PlanliDurusSuresi) * 100, 1)

		PlanlananSure = S20T1.NREF07(i)
		PerformansVerimliligi = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(PlanlananSure, CalismaSuresi) * 100, 1)

		HurdaMiktari = S20T1.NREF08(i)
		KalitePerformansi = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(S20T1.TM_MIKTAR(i), S20T1.TM_MIKTAR(i) + HurdaMiktari) * 100, 1)
		OEE = HazirBulunmaOrani * PerformansVerimliligi * KalitePerformansi / 10000

		MTableT1.SetFieldValue "REPCOL0", -1, OEE
		MTableT2.SetFieldValue "REPCOL0", -1, PerformansVerimliligi
		MTableT3.SetFieldValue "REPCOL0", -1, HazirBulunmaOrani
		MTableT4.SetFieldValue "REPCOL0", -1, KalitePerformansi
	Next

	if MTableT1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(1)
		MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(1)
		MTableT3.SetCurrentRow(1)
		MTableT4.SetCurrentRow(1)
	End If 


	MTableT1.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTableT3.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTableT4.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Function GetDateTimeSerial(Tarih, Saat)
	GetDateTimeSerial = DateSerial(Doc.StrDate_Year(Tarih), Doc.StrDate_Month(Tarih), Doc.StrDate_Day(Tarih))
	GetDateTimeSerial = GetDateTimeSerial + TimeSerial(Doc.CDbl(Left(Saat, 2)), Doc.CDbl(Right(Saat, 2)), 0)
	GetDateTimeSerial = CDbl(GetDateTimeSerial)
End Function
Function PlanliDurusMu(DurmaSebebi, T73T)
	PlanliDurusMu = False
	Set T73 = Doc.Select2("xdxedf", T73T.GetAppName, "DURMA_SEBEBI='" & DurmaSebebi & "'" ,"", -1) 
	If T73.GetRowCount>0 Then 
		PlanliDurusMu = True
	End If
End Function
Function GetPlanlananSure(Miktar, IsNo)
	GetPlanlananSure = 0
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("fsaef34fetdfy", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & IsNo & "'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetPlanlananSure = Doc.Div(M10T.R_MIKTAR0(1) * Miktar, M10T.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(1))
End Function
Function GetHurdaMiktar(EvrakNo, FireTrNum)
	GetHurdaMiktar = 0
	QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & EvrakNo & "'&OR_TRNUM='" & FireTrNum & "'"
	Set S20S = Doc.Select1G("asdfft6u", "SFDC20S", QStr, "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
	if S20S.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetHurdaMiktar = S20S.SF_MIKTAR(1)
End Function

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AT2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : OEE1
Hangi Tablo için : IMLT00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : OEE Hesapla
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Call OEEHesapla()
End Sub
Sub OEEHesapla()
	Set KriterTable = Doc.GetTableObject("TABLO1_KRITERLER")
	QStr = "RECTARIH1>='" & KriterTable.B_TARIH & "'"
	if trim(KriterTable.E_TARIH) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & "&RECTARIH2<='" & Doc.StrDate_Increment(KriterTable.E_TARIH, 1) & "'"
	End if

	BaslangicSerial = GetDateTimeSerial(KriterTable.B_TARIH, KriterTable.BASLANGIC_SAATI)
	if trim(KriterTable.E_TARIH) <> "" Then
		BitisSerial = GetDateTimeSerial(Doc.StrDate_Increment(KriterTable.E_TARIH,1), KriterTable.BASLANGIC_SAATI)
	End if

	Set T73T = Doc.Select1("der5ggt5", "TABL73T", "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&STATU='P'", "", -1)
	Set S20T = Doc.Select1("sefw4fsrtsdty", "SFDC20T", QStr, "", -1)
	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Üretim Kayýtlarý Oluþturuluyor : " & i & " / " & S20T.GetRowCount()
		S20T.NREF01(i) = GetDateTimeSerial(S20T.RECTARIH1(i), S20T.RECTIME1(i))
		S20T.NREF02(i) = GetDateTimeSerial(S20T.RECTARIH2(i), S20T.RECTIME2(i))
		if S20T.RECTIME2(i) <= KriterTable.BASLANGIC_SAATI Then
			S20T.RECTARIH1(i) = Doc.StrDate_Increment(S20T.RECTARIH1(i), -1)
		End if
		if S20T.D7_ISLEM_KODU(i) = "U" Then
			S20T.NREF03(i) = S20T.SURE(i)
		Elseif S20T.D7_ISLEM_KODU(i) = "A" Then
			S20T.NREF04(i) = S20T.SURE(i)
		Elseif S20T.D7_ISLEM_KODU(i) = "D" Then
			if PlanliDurusMu(S20T.DURMA_SEBEBI(i), T73T) Then
				S20T.NREF05(i) = S20T.SURE(i)
			Else
				S20T.NREF06(i) = S20T.SURE(i)
			End if
		End if
		S20T.NREF07(i) = GetPlanlananSure(S20T.TM_MIKTAR(i), S20T.JOBNO(i))
		S20T.NREF08(i) = GetHurdaMiktar(S20T.EVRAKNO(i), S20T.SFDC20S_OR_TRNUM(i))
	Next
	QStr1 = "NREF01>=" & BaslangicSerial
	if trim(KriterTable.E_TARIH) <> "" Then
		QStr1 = QStr1 & "&NREF02<=" & BitisSerial
	End if
	Set S20T1 = Doc.Select2G("sdfeff4tyyh", S20T.GetAppName, QStr1, "KOD+RECTARIH1", "KOD+RECTARIH1", -1)
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("TABLO1")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("TABLO2")
	Set MTableT3 = Doc.GetTableObject("TABLO3")
	Set MTableT4 = Doc.GetTableObject("TABLO4")

	MTableT1.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT1.Empty

	MTableT2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT2.Empty

	MTableT3.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT3.Empty

	MTableT4.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT4.Empty

	For i = 1 to S20T1.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "OEE Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S20T1.GetRowCount()
		if S20T1.KOD(i) <> EskiKod Then
			satirNo = MTableT1.AddRow
			MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			MTableT1.KOD = S20T1.KOD(i)
			MTableT1.KOD_IMLT00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", S20T1.KOD(i), "")

			satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow
			MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			MTableT2.KOD = S20T1.KOD(i)
			MTableT2.KOD_IMLT00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", S20T1.KOD(i), "")

			satirNo = MTableT3.AddRow
			MTableT3.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			MTableT3.KOD = S20T1.KOD(i)
			MTableT3.KOD_IMLT00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", S20T1.KOD(i), "")

			satirNo = MTableT4.AddRow
			MTableT4.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			MTableT4.KOD = S20T1.KOD(i)
			MTableT4.KOD_IMLT00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", S20T1.KOD(i), "")
		End if
		Gun = Doc.CDateToInt(S20T1.RECTARIH1(i)) - Doc.CDateToInt(KriterTable.B_TARIH) + 1
		if Gun >= 1 and Gun <= 40 Then
			PlanliDurusSuresi = 	S20T1.NREF05(i)
			PlanSizDurusSuresi = S20T1.NREF06(i)
			CalismaSuresi = S20T1.NREF03(i)
			HazirBulunmaOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(CalismaSuresi, S20T1.SURE(i) - PlanliDurusSuresi) * 100, 1)

			PlanlananSure = S20T1.NREF07(i)
			PerformansVerimliligi = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(PlanlananSure, CalismaSuresi) * 100, 1)

			HurdaMiktari = S20T1.NREF08(i)
			KalitePerformansi = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(S20T1.TM_MIKTAR(i), S20T1.TM_MIKTAR(i) + HurdaMiktari) * 100, 1)
			OEE = HazirBulunmaOrani * PerformansVerimliligi * KalitePerformansi / 10000

			MTableT1.SetFieldValue "REPCOL" & Gun, -1, OEE
			MTableT2.SetFieldValue "REPCOL" & Gun, -1, PerformansVerimliligi
			MTableT3.SetFieldValue "REPCOL" & Gun, -1, HazirBulunmaOrani
			MTableT4.SetFieldValue "REPCOL" & Gun, -1, KalitePerformansi
		End if

		EskiKod = S20T1.KOD(i)
	Next

	S20T1.GroupBy("KOD")
	For i = 1 to S20T1.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Dönem Toplamlarý Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S20T1.GetRowCount()
		For j = 1 to MTableT1.GetRowCount()
			if trim(MTableT1.KOD(j)) = trim(S20T1.KOD(i)) Then
				satirNo = i
				Exit For
			End if
		Next
		MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT3.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT4.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		PlanliDurusSuresi = 	S20T1.NREF05(i)
		PlanSizDurusSuresi = S20T1.NREF06(i)
		CalismaSuresi = S20T1.NREF03(i)
		HazirBulunmaOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(CalismaSuresi, S20T1.SURE(i) - PlanliDurusSuresi) * 100, 1)

		PlanlananSure = S20T1.NREF07(i)
		PerformansVerimliligi = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(PlanlananSure, CalismaSuresi) * 100, 1)

		HurdaMiktari = S20T1.NREF08(i)
		KalitePerformansi = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(S20T1.TM_MIKTAR(i), S20T1.TM_MIKTAR(i) + HurdaMiktari) * 100, 1)
		OEE = HazirBulunmaOrani * PerformansVerimliligi * KalitePerformansi / 10000

		MTableT1.SetFieldValue "REPCOL0", -1, OEE
		MTableT2.SetFieldValue "REPCOL0", -1, PerformansVerimliligi
		MTableT3.SetFieldValue "REPCOL0", -1, HazirBulunmaOrani
		MTableT4.SetFieldValue "REPCOL0", -1, KalitePerformansi
	Next


	if MTableT1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(1)
		MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(1)
		MTableT3.SetCurrentRow(1)
		MTableT4.SetCurrentRow(1)
	End If 


	MTableT1.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTableT3.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTableT4.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Function GetDateTimeSerial(Tarih, Saat)
	GetDateTimeSerial = DateSerial(Doc.StrDate_Year(Tarih), Doc.StrDate_Month(Tarih), Doc.StrDate_Day(Tarih))
	GetDateTimeSerial = GetDateTimeSerial + TimeSerial(Doc.CDbl(Left(Saat, 2)), Doc.CDbl(Right(Saat, 2)), 0)
	GetDateTimeSerial = CDbl(GetDateTimeSerial)
End Function
Function PlanliDurusMu(DurmaSebebi, T73T)
	PlanliDurusMu = False
	Set T73 = Doc.Select2("xdxedf", T73T.GetAppName, "DURMA_SEBEBI='" & DurmaSebebi & "'" ,"", -1) 
	If T73.GetRowCount>0 Then 
		PlanliDurusMu = True
	End If
End Function
Function GetPlanlananSure(Miktar, IsNo)
	GetPlanlananSure = 0
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("fsaef34fetdfy", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & IsNo & "'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetPlanlananSure = Doc.Div(M10T.R_MIKTAR0(1) * Miktar, M10T.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(1))
End Function
Function GetHurdaMiktar(EvrakNo, FireTrNum)
	GetHurdaMiktar = 0
	QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & EvrakNo & "'&OR_TRNUM='" & FireTrNum & "'"
	Set S20S = Doc.Select1G("asdfft6u", "SFDC20S", QStr, "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
	if S20S.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetHurdaMiktar = S20S.SF_MIKTAR(1)
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	MsgBox GirisMakaralariHatali()
End Sub
Function GirisMakaralariHatali()
	GirisMakaralariHatali = False
	SFDC31T.SafePack()
	For i = 1 to SFDC31T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(SFDC31T.KOD(i)) <> trim(SFDC31E.STOK_CODE) and not HammaddeKoduMu(SFDC31T.KOD(i)) Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Giriþ Makarasý hatalý", "Dikkat", MB_OK+MB_ICONWARNING
			GirisMakaralariHatali = True
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function HammaddeKoduMu(StokKodu)
	HammaddeKoduMu = True
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("dr5y78k", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & SFDC31E.MPSNO & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_KAYNAKKODU='" & StokKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then HammaddeKoduMu = False
End Function

Firma Kodu : AT2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S

Hangi Tablo için : PERSM0

Sub IseGirisBilgileriDoldur(ByVal ExcelSheet,ByVal Satir,ByVal PersonelNo)
	Dim Tbl_PERSMBT2
	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_CALISMA_UCRET_YENI_GIRIS_BASLA"
	Set Tbl_PERSMBT2=Doc.GetTableObject("PERSM0BT2")

	Dim AlanIsmi
	Dim AlanDegeri
	Dim Sutun
	For Sutun=2 To 100
		AlanIsmi=Trim(ExcelSheet.Cells(3, Sutun))
		AlanDegeri=ExcelSheet.Cells(Satir, Sutun)
		If AlanIsmi<>"" Then
			Tbl_PERSMBT2.SetFieldValue AlanIsmi,0,AlanDegeri
		End If
	Next

	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_CALISMA_UCRET_YENI_GIRIS_OK"
End Sub


Sub Makro1()
	Dim myExcel
	Dim mySheet
	Set myExcel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")

	myExcel.Workbooks.Open ("C:\CALISMA.XLS")
	Set mySheet = myExcel.Workbooks("CALISMA.XLS").Worksheets("CALISMA")

	Dim PersonelNo

	Dim I		
	For I=4 To 1000
		PersonelNo=Trim(mySheet.Cells(I, 1))
		If PersonelNo<>"" Then
			PersonelNo=Doc.LeftJustify(PersonelNo,16)
			Doc.Load_Voucher PersonelNo
			If Trim(Doc.GetLoadedVoucher())=Trim(PersonelNo) Then
				Call IseGirisBilgileriDoldur(mySheet,I,PersonelNo)
				Doc.Save_Voucher
			Else
				MsgBox(Trim(PersonelNo) & " Nolu Personel Bulunamadý")
			End If
		End If
	Next

	myExcel.Quit

	Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
